Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on harmonic structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 436. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 778 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the harmonic space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 436 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 778. QED.
